data-role="countrycode" type="hidden" value="GEOIP_COUNTRY_CODE"/> <input data-role="creditcardlink" type="hidden" value="https://www.dealmoon.ca/credit-cards"/> <script type="text/javascript">
var cookieUtil = (function () {
    function read(name) {
        var cookieValue;
        var nameEQ = name + "=";
        var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
        for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
            var c = ca[i];
            while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
                c = c.substring(1, c.length);
            }

            if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) {
                cookieValue = c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
                //解决在tomcat下cookie前面带引号的问题
                if (cookieValue.indexOf("\"") == 0)
                    cookieValue = cookieValue.substring(1, cookieValue.length - 1);
                return decodeURIComponent(cookieValue);
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    function create(name,value,exDays) {
        var domain = '.' + location.host.split('.').splice(1).join('.');
        exDays = exDays || 30;
        var exp = new Date();
        exp.setTime(exp.getTime() + exDays*24*60*60*1000);
        document.cookie = name + "="+ escape (value) + ";expires=" + exp.toGMTString() + "; path=/; domain=" + domain;
    }

    function remove(name) {
        this.create(name, "", -1);
    }

    return {
        read: read,
        create: create,
        remove: remove
    }
})();

(function(){
    var countryCode = 'GEOIP_COUNTRY_CODE' || '';
    var creditCardLink = "https://www.dealmoon.ca/credit-cards";
    var domain = document.domain;
    var link = {
        cn:'https://cn.dealmoon.com/event/cmbc',
        en:creditCardLink
    };
    var $creditTab = document.querySelector('#creditcard');
    if ($creditTab == null) {
        return;
    }
    if(countryCode.toUpperCase() && countryCode.toUpperCase() == 'CN'){
        //如果中国
        if (cookieUtil.read('selectedCreditJump') == '' || cookieUtil.read('selectedCreditJump') == null) {
            //没有cookie弹框选择
            $creditTab.querySelector('a').removeAttribute('href');
            $creditTab.querySelector('a').style.cursor = 'pointer';
        }else{
            //记录了cookie，则直接替换链接为cookie的
            $creditTab.querySelector('a').setAttribute('href',cookieUtil.read('selectedCreditJump'));
        }
    }else{
        //如果是外国
        cookieUtil.remove();
        $creditTab.querySelector('a').setAttribute('href',link.en);
    }
})();
